<script>
(function() {
  'use strict';

  var tagged = false;
  var tries = 0;
  var MAX_TRIES = 600; // ~10s at 60fps

  function tagLogin() {
    if (tagged) return;
    tries++;

    // Try multiple selectors to catch the Login link wherever it lives
    var loginLink =
      document.querySelector('#menu-item-18 > a') ||
      document.querySelector('a[href="https://my.sportmonks.com/login"]') ||
      document.querySelector('a[href*="my.sportmonks.com/login"]');

    if (loginLink) {
      loginLink.id = 'nav-login-link';
      loginLink.setAttribute('data-track', 'nav-login-link');
      loginLink.setAttribute('data-event', 'click_nav_login');
      tagged = true;
      return;
    }
    if (tries < MAX_TRIES) {
      requestAnimationFrame(tagLogin);
    }
  }

  requestAnimationFrame(tagLogin);
})();

  H1 puts the category head term "Sports Data API" in the most
  weighted element. IMPORTANT: this is the homepage, not /football-api/.
  Do NOT add "football api" to the H1/title, target the multi-sport
  category term and let block 00b + the sport pages own the verticals.

  Canonical figures: 2,200+ leagues, 99.99% uptime. Palette: pink +
  greens. No em/en dashes as sentence punctuation. Namespace sm-hmh.

<section class="sm-hmh-hero">
  <canvas class="sm-hmh-bg" id="sm-hmh-bg-canvas" aria-hidden="true"></canvas>
  <div class="sm-hmh-wrap">

<!--
  Hero background animation, variant B ("3b · Live JSON feed") ported from the
  Hero Background Animations design doc. Vanilla canvas, self-contained, no libraries.
  Raining Sportmonks JSON tokens + materialising real fixture API responses, dimmed
  toward centre by the vignette so the hero copy stays legible. Reduced-motion gated.
  Palette: pink #FF0F50 / green #4ED8A0 / cyan / mint. No yellow/gold.
-->
<script>
(function(){
  var canvas = document.getElementById('sm-hmh-bg-canvas');
  if (!canvas || !canvas.getContext) return;
  if (window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) return;
  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  var W = 0, H = 0, dpr = 1, lastW = 0;
  function resize(){
    dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
    W = canvas.clientWidth || 1280; H = canvas.clientHeight || 600;
    canvas.width = W * dpr; canvas.height = H * dpr;
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
  }
  resize();
  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);

  var tokens = ['"fixture_id"','18535517','"league"','"Premier League"','"status"','"LIVE"','"minute"','67','"home_score"','"away_score"','"xG"','1.84','"possession"','58','"shots"','"GOAL"','90','"season"','"2025/26"','"venue"','"assist"','"corners"','"passes"','"result_info"'];
  var tokColor = function(s){ return s[0] === '"' ? (/[0-9]/.test(s) ? 'rgba(24,147,193,ALPHA)' : 'rgba(78,216,160,ALPHA)') : 'rgba(24,147,193,ALPHA)'; };
  var payloads = [
    { req: 'GET /v3/football/fixtures/18535517', lines: ['{','  "fixture_id": 18535517,','  "league": "Premier League",','  "status": "LIVE",','  "minute": 67,','  "score": { "home": 2, "away": 1 },','  "goals": [','    { "player": "Haaland", "minute": 23 },','    { "player": "Saka", "minute": 61 }','  ]','}'] },
    { req: 'GET /v3/football/livescores/inplay', lines: ['{','  "fixture": "Real Madrid vs Barcelona",','  "status": "LIVE",','  "minute": 78,','  "score": { "home": 1, "away": 1 },','  "statistics": {','    "possession": { "home": 54, "away": 46 },','    "shots_on_target": { "home": 5, "away": 4 }','  }','}'] },
    { req: 'GET /v3/football/fixtures/date/2026-07-21', lines: ['{','  "fixture_id": 19204413,','  "league": "Eredivisie",','  "status": "FT",','  "score": { "home": 3, "away": 0 },','  "xG": { "home": 2.41, "away": 0.63 },','  "events": [','    { "type": "GOAL", "minute": 12 },','    { "type": "CARD", "minute": 44 }','  ]','}'] } ];
  var pIdx = 0;
  var fs = 15, lh = 21, chw = 9.6;
  var cols = [];
  function rebuild(){
    cols = [];
    var n = Math.max(2, Math.floor(W / 150));
    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) cols.push({ x: 30 + i * (W - 60) / (n - 1), y: -Math.random() * H, sp: 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.4, rows: Array.from({length: 22}, function(){ return tokens[(Math.random()*tokens.length)|0]; }) });
  }
  var snip = { x: 0, y: 0, chars: 0, phase: 'in', hold: 0, lines: payloads[0].lines, req: payloads[0].req };
  snip.place = function(){
    pIdx = (pIdx + 1) % payloads.length;
    snip.lines = payloads[pIdx].lines; snip.req = payloads[pIdx].req;
    var w = Math.max.apply(null, snip.lines.map(function(l){ return l.length; })) * chw;
    var left = Math.random() < 0.5;
    snip.x = left ? W * 0.05 : Math.min(W * 0.62, W - w - 40);
    snip.y = H * (0.14 + Math.random()*0.38); snip.chars = 0; snip.phase = 'in'; snip.hold = 0;
  };
  var t = 0;
  function colourFor(ln, idx){
    var before = ln.slice(0, idx + 1);
    var inStr = (before.match(/"/g) || []).length % 2 === 1;
    if (inStr) {
      var close = ln.indexOf('"', before.lastIndexOf('"') + 1);
      var isKey = close > -1 && /^\s*:/.test(ln.slice(close + 1));
      return isKey ? '78,216,160' : '255,15,80';
    }
    if (/[0-9]/.test(ln[idx])) return '24,147,193';
    return '150,165,190';
  }
  function frame(){
    if (W !== lastW) { rebuild(); lastW = W; }
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
    ctx.font = '500 ' + fs + 'px "JetBrains Mono", ui-monospace, monospace';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'top';
    var cx = W / 2;
    cols.forEach(function(c){
      c.y += c.sp; if (c.y > H) { c.y = -c.rows.length * lh; c.rows = c.rows.map(function(){ return tokens[(Math.random()*tokens.length)|0]; }); }
      var centreFade = Math.min(1, Math.abs(c.x - cx) / (W * 0.32));
      c.rows.forEach(function(tok, r){
        var y = c.y + r * lh; if (y < -lh || y > H) return;
        var head = r === c.rows.length - 1;
        var a = (head ? 0.42 : 0.20 * (r / c.rows.length + 0.3)) * (0.22 + 0.78 * centreFade);
        ctx.fillStyle = (head ? 'rgba(255,255,255,A)' : tokColor(tok)).replace('ALPHA', a.toFixed(3)).replace('A', a.toFixed(3));
        ctx.fillText(tok, c.x, y);
      });
    });
    var total = snip.lines.join('\n').length;
    if (snip.phase === 'in') { snip.chars += 2.4; if (snip.chars >= total) { snip.chars = total; snip.phase = 'hold'; } }
    else if (snip.phase === 'hold') { snip.hold += 1; if (snip.hold > 170) snip.phase = 'out'; }
    else { snip.chars -= 7; if (snip.chars <= 0) snip.place(); }
    var shown = Math.max(0, snip.chars | 0);
    var blockW = Math.max.apply(null, snip.lines.map(function(l){ return l.length; })) * chw;
    var centreFadeS = Math.min(1, Math.abs(snip.x + blockW/2 - cx) / (W * 0.30));
    var baseA = 0.62 * (0.3 + 0.7 * centreFadeS);
    var reveal = snip.phase === 'out' ? Math.min(1, snip.chars / 12) : 1;
    ctx.font = '700 13px "JetBrains Mono", ui-monospace, monospace';
    var hy = snip.y - 30;
    var method = snip.req.split(' ')[0], path = snip.req.split(' ').slice(1).join(' ');
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,15,80,' + (baseA*reveal).toFixed(3) + ')';
    ctx.fillText(method, snip.x, hy);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + (baseA*0.7*reveal).toFixed(3) + ')';
    ctx.fillText(path, snip.x + (method.length + 1) * 8, hy);
    if (snip.phase !== 'in' || shown >= total) {
      var chipX = snip.x, chipY = snip.y - 6;
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,229,160,' + (0.14*reveal).toFixed(3) + ')';
      ctx.fillRect(chipX, chipY, 68, 18);
      ctx.font = '700 11px "JetBrains Mono", ui-monospace, monospace';
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,229,160,' + (baseA*reveal).toFixed(3) + ')';
      ctx.fillText('200 OK', chipX + 8, chipY + 3);
    }
    ctx.font = '500 16px "JetBrains Mono", ui-monospace, monospace';
    var bodyY = snip.y + 20;
    var used = 0;
    snip.lines.forEach(function(ln, i){
      var take = Math.max(0, Math.min(ln.length, shown - used)); used += ln.length + 1;
      for (var k = 0; k < take; k++) {
        var ch = ln[k]; if (ch === ' ') continue;
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(' + colourFor(ln, k) + ',' + (baseA*reveal).toFixed(3) + ')';
        ctx.fillText(ch, snip.x + k * chw, bodyY + i * 22);
      }
    });
    if (snip.phase === 'in' && Math.floor(t*4)%2) {
      var li = 0, acc = 0; for (var i2=0;i2<snip.lines.length;i2++){ if (acc + snip.lines[i2].length >= shown){ li=i2; break;} acc += snip.lines[i2].length+1; }
      var col = Math.max(0, shown - acc);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + baseA.toFixed(3) + ')';
      ctx.fillRect(snip.x + col*chw, bodyY + li*22, 8, 16);
    }
    t += 0.016;
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  frame();
})();
</script>

<script>
(function(){
  var root = document.getElementById('smx-root'); if (!root) return;
  (function(){
    function isLight(bg){ var m=(bg||'').match(/\d+(\.\d+)?/g); if(!m) return false;
      var r=+m[0],g=+m[1],b=+m[2],a=(m[3]!==undefined?+m[3]:1); return a>0.5 && r>=232 && g>=232 && b>=232; }
    var el=root;
    for (var i=0;i<9 && el && el!==document.documentElement;i++){ el=el.parentElement; if(!el) break; if(el.tagName==='BODY') break;
      if(isLight(getComputedStyle(el).backgroundColor)) el.style.setProperty('background','#01002C','important'); }
  })();
  var btns = root.querySelectorAll('.smx-btn'); var panes = root.querySelectorAll('.smx-pane');
  btns.forEach(function(btn){ btn.addEventListener('click', function(){
    var sport = btn.getAttribute('data-sport');
    btns.forEach(function(b){ b.classList.toggle('is-active', b===btn); });
    panes.forEach(function(p){ p.classList.toggle('is-active', p.getAttribute('data-sport')===sport); });
  }); });
  root.querySelectorAll('.sm-mc-split').forEach(function(split){
    var handle = split.querySelector('.sm-mc-handle');
    if (handle){ var dragging=false;
      function setSplit(pct){ pct=Math.max(20,Math.min(80,pct)); split.style.setProperty('--mc-left',pct+'fr'); split.style.setProperty('--mc-right',(100-pct)+'fr'); }
      function onMove(x){ var r=split.getBoundingClientRect(); setSplit(((x-r.left)/r.width)*100); }
      handle.addEventListener('mousedown',function(e){dragging=true;split.classList.add('dragging');e.preventDefault();});
      document.addEventListener('mousemove',function(e){if(dragging)onMove(e.clientX);});
      document.addEventListener('mouseup',function(){dragging=false;split.classList.remove('dragging');});
      handle.addEventListener('touchstart',function(){dragging=true;split.classList.add('dragging');},{passive:true});
      document.addEventListener('touchmove',function(e){if(dragging&&e.touches[0])onMove(e.touches[0].clientX);},{passive:true});
      document.addEventListener('touchend',function(){dragging=false;split.classList.remove('dragging');});
      handle.addEventListener('dblclick',function(){setSplit(50);});
    }
    var tabs=split.querySelectorAll('.sm-mc-tab'); var panels=split.querySelectorAll('.sm-mc-panel');
    tabs.forEach(function(tab){ tab.addEventListener('click', function(){
      tabs.forEach(function(t){t.classList.remove('is-active');}); panels.forEach(function(pp){pp.classList.remove('is-active');});
      tab.classList.add('is-active'); var sel=split.querySelector('[data-panel="'+tab.getAttribute('data-tab')+'"]'); if(sel)sel.classList.add('is-active');
    }); });
    split.querySelectorAll('img').forEach(function(img){ img.addEventListener('error',function(){ img.style.background='rgba(255,255,255,.08)'; img.removeAttribute('src'); }); });
  });
})();
</script>

<!--
  LIGHTBOX GUARD (part 3). Second layer behind .sm-pl-tg-logo{pointer-events:none}.
  Runs on the CAPTURE phase so it fires before any lightbox listener bound inside
  the grid, and stops that listener from running at all. It deliberately does NOT
  cancel the event or set a redirect, so the browser follows the link natively.
  Without both layers a logo click opens the logo as an image overlay instead of
  following the link. Keep both if this grid is copied to another page.
-->
<script>
(function(){
  var grid = document.querySelector('.sm-pl-tg-grid');
  if (!grid) return;
  grid.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var t = e.target;
    if (t && t.tagName === 'IMG') { e.preventDefault(); e.stopImmediatePropagation(); return; }
    if (t && t.closest && t.closest('a.sm-pl-tg-cell-link')) { e.stopImmediatePropagation(); }
  }, true);
})();
</script>
                                <div class="button-wrapper">
                                                        </div>

    <div class="sm-head">
      <div class="sm-eyebrow">The system</div>
      <h2>One platform that turns raw matches into data you can ship</h2>
      <p class="sm-lede">Behind every endpoint is a full pipeline: collect it, verify it with humans and machines, serve it in one uniform format, and cover it at the scale of a big provider. Here is what you are actually building on.</p>
    </div>

    <!-- ROW 1: verified pipeline (text left, visual right) -->
    <div class="sm-row">
      <div class="sm-txt">
        <div class="sm-kicker" style="color:#FF0F50;"><span class="kd" style="background:#FF0F50;"></span>Verified data pipeline</div>
        <h3>First-hand data, checked by humans and machines.</h3>
        <p>Scores, events and stats come straight from our own in-house scouts and official data partners across football, cricket and Formula 1. Every feed is then reconciled automatically and reviewed by a real operations team, 24 hours a day, so when a source disagrees with itself it gets caught before it reaches your app.</p>
        <p>The result is 99.99% uptime and 6.4 billion API requests served every month, for 10 years running.</p>
        <div class="sm-tag">Ship data your users can trust on matchday.</div>
      </div>
      <div class="sm-vis">
        <div class="sm-card">
          <div class="sm-card-head"><span class="sm-dot r"></span><span class="sm-dot"></span><span class="sm-dot g"></span><span class="sm-badge">Sportmonks · ingestion monitor</span></div>

            <div class="sm-mon-sub">6.4B requests / month · rolling 30 days</div>

        <div class="sm-kicker" style="color:#FF0F50;"><span class="kd" style="background:#FF0F50;"></span>One API, one format</div>
        <h3>Learn it once. Use it across every sport.</h3>
        <p>Football, cricket and Formula 1 share the same authentication, the same response shape and the same query language. Attach exactly the data you need with a single <span class="sm-inc">?include=</span>, no extra round trips.</p>
        <p>Professional-grade coverage of squads, stats, lineups, xG, predictions and odds, all in one predictable structure your code already understands.</p>

<div class="sm-term"><span class="ln"><span class="cmt"># same shape, any sport</span></span><span class="ln"><span class="p">GET</span> /v3/football/fixtures/<span class="n">18535517</span></span><span class="ln">     ?include=<span class="inc">events;lineups;statistics</span></span><span class="ln"> </span><span class="ln"><span class="ok">200 OK</span> · 41 ms</span><span class="ln">{</span><span class="ln">  <span class="k">"data"</span>: {</span><span class="ln">    <span class="k">"name"</span>: <span class="s">"Man City vs Arsenal"</span>,</span><span class="ln">    <span class="k">"statistics"</span>: [{ <span class="k">"xg"</span>: <span class="n">2.14</span> }],</span><span class="ln">    <span class="k">"events"</span>: [{ <span class="k">"player"</span>: <span class="s">"Haaland"</span> }]</span><span class="ln">  }</span><span class="ln">}</span><span class="ln"> </span><span class="ln"><span class="cmt"># swap the sport, keep the pattern</span></span><span class="ln"><span class="p">GET</span> /v3/cricket/fixtures ?include=<span class="inc">scoreboards</span></span><span class="ln"><span class="p">GET</span> /v3/formula1/races  ?include=<span class="inc">results</span></span></div>

<script>
(function(){
  var root = document.querySelector('.sm-hmsee');
  if (!root) return;
  var tabs = root.querySelectorAll('.sm-tab');
  var panels = root.querySelectorAll('.sm-panel');
  panels.forEach(function(p){ p.setAttribute('role','tabpanel'); });
  function select(tab){
    var key = tab.getAttribute('data-tab');
    tabs.forEach(function(t){ var on = t === tab; t.classList.toggle('is-active', on); t.setAttribute('aria-selected', on ? 'true' : 'false'); });
    panels.forEach(function(p){ p.classList.toggle('is-active', p.getAttribute('data-panel') === key); });
  }
  tabs.forEach(function(tab){
    tab.setAttribute('aria-selected', tab.classList.contains('is-active') ? 'true' : 'false');
    tab.addEventListener('click', function(){
      select(tab);
      var strip = root.querySelector('.sm-tabs');
      if (strip) { strip.scrollTo({ left: tab.offsetLeft - (strip.clientWidth - tab.offsetWidth) / 2, behavior:'smooth' }); }
    });
  });

  // prev/next arrow scrolling
  var strip = root.querySelector('.sm-tabs');
  var prev = root.querySelector('.sm-tabnav.prev');
  var next = root.querySelector('.sm-tabnav.next');
  function syncNav(){
    if (!strip || !prev || !next) return;
    var max = strip.scrollWidth - strip.clientWidth - 2;
    prev.disabled = strip.scrollLeft <= 2;
    next.disabled = strip.scrollLeft >= max;
  }
  if (strip && prev && next){
    prev.addEventListener('click', function(){ strip.scrollBy({ left:-220, behavior:'smooth' }); });
    next.addEventListener('click', function(){ strip.scrollBy({ left:220, behavior:'smooth' }); });
    strip.addEventListener('scroll', function(){ window.requestAnimationFrame(syncNav); });
    window.addEventListener('resize', syncNav);
    syncNav();
  }
  // graceful fallback if a CDN crest/photo fails to load
  root.querySelectorAll('.sm-mc img').forEach(function(img){
    img.addEventListener('error', function(){ img.style.visibility = 'hidden'; });
  });
})();
</script></section>

  ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  HOMEPAGE DRAFT 2, BLOCK 06 (REPLACEMENT): PROOF BY NUMBERS
  ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  Replaces DRAFT-06-testimonials.html (the "Builders on Sportmonks"
  carousel) in the DRAFT2 paste order. Modelled on the "Here's some
  numbers" proof block: a row of customer stat cards on top, then a
  row of correctly-attributed quote cards.

  Why the swap (21 Jul 2026, Wesley):
  - The carousel repeated the same "feels like a partner" quote under
    two different ScoutsLand names (Egle Neumann vs Ahmet Sayarlioglu),
    the July 20 recheck section 2.4 attribution bug. This block drops
    that disputed quote and uses one clean, correctly-attributed line
    per customer.
  - Numbers-first proof reads faster than a rotating carousel.

  Data provenance:
  - Stat cards use curated case-study figures already shipped in the
    trusted-by grid (Botbrains 60% faster dev, Fine Line 15K+ users,
    BF Bot Manager 7+ years, Fotboll 60K fans/mo) plus the canonical
    Sportmonks scale stat (6.4B requests/mo, positioning-framework.md).
  - Quotes are verbatim from customer-proof.md / the shipped
    testimonials source. Nothing invented.

  Rules honored: eyebrow #FF0F50; palette pink + greens + indigo only
  (no yellow/gold); no em/en dashes as sentence punctuation; the real
  numbers live in the static DOM (the count-up script only re-animates
  them at runtime, gated by prefers-reduced-motion) so crawlers/AI
  engines still read the final values; UNIQUE class prefix hmn- (never
  bare sm-*, per 2026-07-06 learning on theme class collisions).
  Stat cards use a fixed label height so the number, the divider line
  and the source label align across all five cards regardless of how
  many lines the label wraps to. Width 1140px.
  Pillar fit: P1 (reliability/scale) + P3 (real builders, the "face").
  ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

<script>
(function(){
  var root = document.querySelector('.hmn');
  if(!root) return;
  var stats = root.querySelector('.hmn-stats');
  if(!stats) return;
  var reduced = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  // Parse each number into {numeric target, decimal places, trailing suffix}.
  // The real value stays in the DOM; we only replace it at animation time.
  var items = [];
  root.querySelectorAll('.hmn-num').forEach(function(el){
    var raw = el.textContent.trim();
    var m = raw.match(/^(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)(.*)$/);
    if(!m){ return; }
    items.push({
      el: el,
      raw: raw,
      target: parseFloat(m[1]),
      decimals: (m[1].split('.')[1] || '').length,
      suffix: m[2]
    });
  });
  if(!items.length || reduced) return; // reduced motion: leave the real numbers untouched
  var done = false;
  function animate(){
    if(done) return; done = true;
    var dur = 1300, start = null;
    function frame(ts){
      if(start === null) start = ts;
      var t = Math.min((ts - start) / dur, 1);
      var eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
      items.forEach(function(p){
        p.el.textContent = (p.target * eased).toFixed(p.decimals) + p.suffix;
      });
      if(t < 1){ requestAnimationFrame(frame); }
      else { items.forEach(function(p){ p.el.textContent = p.raw; }); }
    }
    // Zero out just before counting so there is no visible flash of the final value.
    items.forEach(function(p){ p.el.textContent = (0).toFixed(p.decimals) + p.suffix; });
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  if('IntersectionObserver' in window){
    var io = new IntersectionObserver(function(es){
      es.forEach(function(e){ if(e.isIntersecting){ animate(); io.disconnect(); } });
    }, { threshold: 0.4 });
    io.observe(stats);
  } else {
    animate();
  }
})();
</script></section>

  to find it). This block puts the P2 signal on the page: transparent,
  free-to-start, published prices, with a "from" price per sport and a
  link to each pricing page. Framing per positioning: approachable, not
  "cheap". Prices verified from live pages 8 Jul 2026.
    Football  from €29/mo   /football-api/plans-pricing/
    Cricket   from €29/mo   /cricket-api/#plans
    F1        from €55/mo   /formula-one-api/#formula-1-plan   (yearly; €65 monthly)
  Pillar fit: P2. Namespace sm-hmpr. Width: 900px.

-->

  <div class="sm-inner">
    <div class="sm-eyebrow">What is Sportmonks</div>
    <h2 id="sm-hmwis-h2">The most approachable sports data API</h2>
    <p>Sportmonks is a sports data API that gives developers and companies live scores, statistics, lineups, xG, odds and predictions for football, cricket and Formula 1 through one uniform REST API returning JSON. It covers 2,200+ football leagues and cups, 140+ cricket leagues, and the full Formula 1 season, with 99.99% uptime and 6.4 billion requests served every month. More than 30,000 builders and 1,200 companies use it to power live-score apps, fantasy games, betting products, analytics tools and media sites. Unlike enterprise feeds that are priced out of reach, or budget APIs that break under load, Sportmonks pairs professional-grade data with transparent pricing, a free plan, and human support 7 days a week.</p>
    <p>Start on the <a href="https://www.sportmonks.com/football-api/">Football API</a>, <a href="https://www.sportmonks.com/cricket-api/">Cricket API</a> or <a href="https://www.sportmonks.com/formula-one-api/">Formula 1 API</a>, or read the <a href="https://docs.sportmonks.com/football/">developer docs</a>.</p>
    <div class="sm-facts">
      <span class="sm-fact"><b>2,200+</b> football leagues</span>
      <span class="sm-fact"><b>99.99%</b> uptime</span>
      <span class="sm-fact"><b>6.4B</b> requests / month</span>
      <span class="sm-fact"><b>30,000+</b> builders</span>
    </div>
  </div>

<section class="sm-hmfaq" aria-labelledby="sm-hmfaq-h2">
  <div class="sm-inner">

    <div class="sm-head">
      <div class="sm-eyebrow">Sports data API, FAQ</div>
      <h2 id="sm-hmfaq-h2">The questions developers actually ask</h2>
      <p class="sm-lede">Quick answers before you sign up. Want the long version? It's all in the <a href="https://docs.sportmonks.com/football/" style="color:#FF0F50;text-decoration:none;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,15,80,0.4);">developer docs</a>.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="sm-list">

      <details>
        <summary>What is a sports data API?<span class="sm-q-icon" aria-hidden="true">+</span></summary>
        <div class="sm-a">
          <p>A sports data API is a service you call over HTTP to get structured sports data, live scores, fixtures, statistics, lineups, standings, odds and predictions, as JSON your application can use directly, instead of scraping websites or maintaining feeds yourself. Sportmonks is a REST API covering football, cricket and Formula 1, all in one uniform format.</p>
          <p>You send a request with your token, and get back clean data in seconds. See it live in the <a href="https://docs.sportmonks.com/football/">docs</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </details>

      <details>
        <summary>Which sports does Sportmonks cover?<span class="sm-q-icon" aria-hidden="true">+</span></summary>
        <div class="sm-a">
          <p>Three sports today: <a href="https://www.sportmonks.com/football-api/">football</a> (2,200+ leagues and cups worldwide, plus xG, predictions and odds), <a href="https://www.sportmonks.com/cricket-api/">cricket</a> (140+ leagues across 30+ countries with ball-by-ball data), and <a href="https://www.sportmonks.com/formula-one-api/">Formula 1</a> (every race in the season with lap times, drivers, teams and standings).</p>
          <p>Football is the flagship product and the deepest, but all three run on the same API structure, so what you learn on one carries over to the others.</p>
        </div>
      </details>

      <details>
        <summary>Is there a free plan?<span class="sm-q-icon" aria-hidden="true">+</span></summary>
        <div class="sm-a">
          <p>Yes. Create an account on <a href="https://my.sportmonks.com/register">My Sportmonks</a> and you get instant access to a free football plan with full data features on the Danish Superliga and Scottish Premiership, no credit card and no expiry. Every paid plan also comes with a one-time 14-day free trial.</p>
          <p>Either way you'll have a working API token and real data within about five minutes of signing up.</p>
        </div>
      </details>

      <details>
        <summary>What format does the API return, REST, JSON, GraphQL?<span class="sm-q-icon" aria-hidden="true">+</span></summary>
        <div class="sm-a">
          <p>Sportmonks is a REST API returning JSON. Every endpoint supports the <code>?include=</code> parameter, which eager-loads related resources (lineups, events, statistics, odds) on the same request. A typical match-centre call returns one fixture object with everything attached instead of five sequential calls. Filtering and sorting parameters let you request only the fields you need.</p>
          <p>Code examples in JavaScript, Python, PHP and Java, plus Postman collections, are in the <a href="https://docs.sportmonks.com/football/">docs</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </details>

      <details>
        <summary>How reliable is the data?<span class="sm-q-icon" aria-hidden="true">+</span></summary>
        <div class="sm-a">
          <p>Uptime sits at 99.99% across the product, monitored continuously including weekends when most matches are live, and we serve 6.4 billion requests a month. Live events like goals, cards and substitutions typically appear within seconds of the broadcast, and detailed data goes through a 24/7 verification process run by our in-house data ops team plus automated cross-checks, so an event is confirmed before it's published rather than corrected after.</p>
          <p>Current status is always on the <a href="https://www.sportmonks.com/api-status/">API status page</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </details>

      <details>
        <summary>Do I need to be a developer to use it?<span class="sm-q-icon" aria-hidden="true">+</span></summary>
        <div class="sm-a">
          <p>To build directly on the API, yes, you make HTTP requests and parse JSON. If you'd rather not build, we offer plug-and-play <a href="https://www.sportmonks.com/football-api/football-widgets/">widgets</a> for livescores, leagues and teams that you embed with a snippet, no backend and no scraping.</p>
          <p>And whichever path you take, support is human: real people 7 days a week, plus a personal contact once you're on a plan.</p>
        </div>
      </details>

    </div>

  </div>
</section>

<script type="text/javascript" src="https://widgets.sportmonks.com/js/app.js"></script><script id="sm-pricing-save-badge-js">
(function () {
    var BADGE_TEXT = 'Save 20%';

    function injectBadge() {
        var yearly = document.querySelector('.switch__switch--yearly[data-id="yearly"]');
        if (!yearly) return false;
        if (yearly.querySelector('.save-badge')) return true;
        var badge = document.createElement('span');
        badge.className = 'save-badge';
        badge.textContent = BADGE_TEXT;
        yearly.appendChild(badge);
        return true;
    }

    function init() {
        if (injectBadge()) return;
        // Fallback: pricing block rendered after initial paint (lazy load, SPA, etc.)
        var observer = new MutationObserver(function () {
            if (injectBadge()) observer.disconnect();
        });
        observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
        // Safety net: stop watching after 10s so we don't keep an observer alive forever
        setTimeout(function () { observer.disconnect(); }, 10000);
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
    } else {
        init();
    }
})();
</script>

		<script>
		(function() {
			'use strict';
			var parentSelector = '.menu-item.wpml-ls-current-language.menu-item-has-children';
			
			function toggleSubmenu(link, forceClose) {
				var isExpanded = link.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';
				if (forceClose || isExpanded) {
					link.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
				} else {
					link.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
				}
			}
			
			function handleKeydown(e) {
				var key = e.key || e.keyCode;
				if (key === 'Enter' || key === ' ' || key === 13 || key === 32) {
					e.preventDefault();
					toggleSubmenu(this);
				} else if (key === 'Escape' || key === 'Esc' || key === 27) {
					if (this.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true') {
						e.preventDefault();
						toggleSubmenu(this, true);
					}
				}
			}
			
			function handleBlur(e) {
				var link = this;
				var parent = link.closest(parentSelector);
				if (!parent) return;
				
				setTimeout(function() {
					if (!parent.contains(document.activeElement)) {
						toggleSubmenu(link, true);
					}
				}, 100);
			}
			
			function init() {
				var parents = document.querySelectorAll(parentSelector);
				for (var i = 0; i < parents.length; i++) {
					var link = parents[i].querySelector('a[aria-expanded]');
					if (link) {
						link.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeydown);
						link.addEventListener('blur', handleBlur);
					}
				}
			}
			
			if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
				document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
			} else {
				init();
			}
		})();
		</script>
